# Sequential specification progress

Owner: **Make Rimworld Great Again** (`fumblesneeze.megamod`, `mods/megamod`). Target: **1.7 beta**.
Started **2026-11-02**, following the user's instruction to deep-dive **one mod or custom feature at a time**.

This is the refinement-stage work ledger, separate from observable OpenSpec contracts. Research public
documentation first, then source/decompilation for gaps. Record open questions for later instead of
interrupting the work. No runtime implementation or gameplay acceptance is claimed by a draft.

## Status and next work

## Open questions and gaps

Scope disposition,2026-11-08: any older row concerned only with original-mod API/drop-in parity,
consumer contracts, compatibility facades or coexistence matrices is **out of scope**, not an
unanswered user question or an implementation/release blocker. Keep its historical research facts;
do not resume that work. The active requirement is a startup warning and best-effort ordinary
feature-off handling. Genuine external integrations and semantic game-target admission remain due.
See [the governing decision](2026-11-08-drug-policy-additions.md).

## Resolved questions and document review

- **CH-Q1 resolved:** exact 1.7 settings loading returns a fresh 60% object when no record loads;
  a loaded record without the field uses 50%. Megamod explicitly proposes a consistent 60%.
- Independent review of the first three drafts found two omissions, both corrected: the CM
  proportional label health strip and the custom colonist meal-carrying queue entry. The invented
  source name remains excluded; the user's underlying confirmed feature remains selected.
- Independent review of drafts four through six found two weather-document issues, both corrected:
  Use global remains usable in loaded saves, and lower-tech war veils retain the candidate-filter
  restriction found in the source. No substantive storage/growing draft issue was reported.
- Independent review of draft seven identified implicit source differences, now corrected in the
  observable contract: treatment-priority sorting ignores the current tending cooldown; enhanced
  bleeding icons pulse with rate; condition-specific progress patterns are explicitly retained.
- Independent review of draft eight found three omissions, all resolved: relative work ordering at
  equal priorities; removing finish authorization when a target recovers; and the actual broad scope
  of the party-hunter corpse option, including melee/finishing and non-prey kills. Source large-area
  selection, cursor seed feedback and Architect closing are also explicit. No runtime acceptance claimed.
- Independent review of draft nine found **no issues** after checking the intake, foundation,
  comparison contract, coverage and selected native/source paths. Runtime material locking,
  provider compatibility and performance remain QM-Q1–4; no game was launched for prose review.
- Independent review of draft ten found one evidence correction, resolved: the Show Possible
  Materials prefix receives Bill/Rect by name, while Nice Bill Tab's target takes Rect/Bill first.
  No substantive contract issue was found. Installed hashes and representative source/consumer paths
  were checked; no runtime task or compatibility claim was accepted.
- Independent review of draft eleven found one composition conflict, resolved in the spec, design
  and acceptance tasks: preserve a stat sort only while remaining filtered recipes support it;
  hiding the last relevant recipe clears the sort with the S009 explanation. The reviewer checked
  the installed hash and representative settings/hiding/add/shortage/provider paths and found no
  further actionable source-fidelity issue. No game or runtime acceptance was claimed.
- Independent review of draft twelve found one material-transition gap, resolved in the spec,
  design, report and tasks: implicit previews follow deliberate stat/material-mode changes and
  main Add follows the same choice; explicit previews/pinned comparisons remain fixed, and Use list
  choice clears a preview override. Background stock refresh still requires review before material
  substitution. No other actionable issue was found; no game or runtime test was run.
- Independent review of draft fifteen found **no issues**. The reviewer checked installed recipe
  XML/hashes, native non-mixing, bill counting/save references, candidate XML Extensions operations
  and browser/linking composition. BS-Q1–4 retain choices and runtime proof; no game was launched.
- Independent review of draft sixteen found **no issues**. Source defaults, zero/per-cell/shape/size
  behavior, persistence and the actual Vehicle Map Framework ordering contract agreed with the draft.
  Common settings, coexistence and explicit corrections were consistent. DT-Q1–4 retain runtime
  proof; the reviewer did not edit files, run tests or launch the game.
- Source identity coverage check includes all 108 selected identities plus both interview UI references.
- Independent review of draft seventeen found one source-evidence error, corrected in research,
  coverage and design: BWM does expose DrawFilters, and EGO's current adapter extends its target
  controls to person-count modes while excluding surplus. The separately missing old copy bridge
  remains distinct. Installed hash and representative source paths were checked; no other actionable
